## Description

<!-- SECTION:DESCRIPTION:BEGIN -->
The last whole-dashboard live audit (TSO-0022) predates a long run of changes: the tab regroup (TSO-0092), new visualisation types (TSO-0150), HA coordination and ingress WAL panels, and many collector additions. TSO-0170 found the whole Node Metrics tab ignoring the tailnet and provider variables. Nothing caught it because the selector-scope test from TSO-0022 covered only the Policy and Config tabs, so similar gaps may exist on other tabs. This task audits every tab of the Tailnet and Exporter Health dashboards as rendered on the live stack. It produces a findings record and follow-up tasks. The fixes themselves belong in those follow-ups unless one is a single generated-query correction small enough to land here. Dashboards are delivered only through grafana-sync; never push them with gcx. Keep lab names, device names, addresses and raw telemetry values out of every tracked file, this task included.
<!-- SECTION:DESCRIPTION:END -->

## Acceptance Criteria
<!-- AC:BEGIN -->
- [x] #1 Every panel on every tab of both generated dashboards is inventoried with a verdict (correct, misleading, broken, redundant or missing-signal) and a one-line reason, recorded in the task notes without lab identifiers
- [x] #2 Every metric, log and trace query is executed against the live stack over a stated window; each one either returns data or has its absence explained (feature disabled, prerequisite absent, or genuinely zero), and no panel shows a query error
- [x] #3 Each signal the exporter emits (per internal/catalog) reaches a panel or has a documented disposition, and each panel or row whose question no operator would act on is flagged for removal or merge
- [x] #4 Every finding not fixed in this task is filed as its own Backlog task with observable acceptance criteria, and this task lists the IDs it filed
<!-- AC:END -->

## Definition of Done
<!-- DOD:BEGIN -->
- [x] #1 just check passes (the full gate; it is what CI enforces)
- [x] #2 just gen leaves no diff (only if a generated artifact's inputs changed)
- [x] #3 just --fmt --check passes and every new recipe has a # doc comment and a [group(...)]
<!-- DOD:END -->

## Implementation Plan

<!-- SECTION:PLAN:BEGIN -->
Inventory every generated panel and catalog signal; extend selector-scope proof across all tabs with a failing mutation; use root-provided live query and render evidence to assign verdicts and identify follow-ups; return staged candidate for guarded review, serialized gate and landing.

loop27: retained patch verified and applied cleanly to fresh base; finish live absence/control/render evidence, then root review, landing and exact-SHA gates.

loop28: apply verified retained candidate to fresh main worktree, update only D64 tailnet-matcher expectations, reprove selector negative mutation, complete live prerequisite/control/non-Overview evidence, then guarded review and sharded CodeRabbit, staged candidate gate, root landing and composed/CI/sync checks.

loop33 attempt4of5: after connectivity and memory landing, freeze final published dashboard/source identities and >=3h explicit live window; inventory every panel/query/variable, execute all signal queries, causally classify absence from same-image evidence, verify all tabs/controls, file observable follow-ups and retain exact proof boundaries.
<!-- SECTION:PLAN:END -->

At the shipped Inventory and Hygiene table widths, Device key expiry (time until) and Last seen (time since) show metadata columns while their question-answering duration measurements are outside the visible table area. Last seen (time since) visibly formats an OS-version metadata cell with a seconds suffix in the retained live Inventory and Hygiene render. A panel-wide duration unit must not alter metadata presentation. | TSO-0212 (Keep table measurements visible ahead of metadata); TSO-0215 (Keep metadata free of numeric and duration formatting) |

Task: TSO-0172 (comprehensive live dashboard audit).
